We can compute C in vectorized loops:
```
def full_c(h):
 n=len(h); one_h=1-h; C=np.empty(2*n-1)
 C[n-1]=np.dot(h, one_h)
 for m in range(1,n): C[n-1+m]=np.dot(h[:n-m], one_h[m:]) # h_i(1-h_{i+m})
 C[n-1-m]=np.dot(h[m:], one_h[:n-m]) # h_i(1-h_{i-m}), equivalent np.dot(h[m:], one_h[:n-m])
```
This is O(n^2) Python loop 700, each dot length decreasing, sum 245k operations, very fast (maybe 0.01s? Actually 700 Python loops with np.dot ~ maybe 0.001 each ->0.07s; ok). np.correlate may be faster in C. For gradient, similarly vectorized.

We can compute via slicing:
```
if wpos !=0:
  g[:n-m] += wpos*(1-h[m:])
  g[m:] -= wpos*h[:n-m]
if wneg !=0:
  g[m:] += wneg*(1-h[:n-m])
  g[:n-m] -= wneg*h[m:]
```

def project_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2.0
 arr=np.clip(h,0,1)
 def proj(t): return np.clip(arr+t,0,1)
 lo,hi = -arr.min(), 1-arr.max()
 if sum(arr) < target:
   # need t positive; but uniform might not reach if all at1? target<=n so feasible.
   lo=0; hi=1-arr.max() if max<1 else 0; if hi==0 no change
 elif sum>target: lo=arr.min(); hi=0
 for 60: mid; s=sum(proj(mid)); if s<target: lo=mid else hi=mid
 return proj((lo+hi)/2)
```

2. Full C function and gradient:
```
def corr_full(h):
  n=len(h); C=np.empty(2*n-1); one=1-h
  C[n-1]=np.dot(h,one)
  for m in range(1,n):
    C[n-1+m]=np.dot(h[:n-m], one[m:])
    C[n-1-m]=np.dot(h[m:], one[:n-m])
  return C
```

So for full C weights, define w_pos and w_neg arrays length n with:

`w_neg[m] = weights[n-1-m]` for m=0..n-1. But if we include m=0 in both, double. Set `w_neg[0]=0`. Then total w_total = w_pos + w_neg? For positive lags, generic formula G_plus(w_pos) as above.
For negative lags, G_neg from weights w_neg (m≥1) where contribution = prefix_neg? Derive analogous:
- First term (1-h[p-m]) valid m≤p: sum_{m=0}^p w_neg[m] = prefix_w[p].
- Second term h[p-m] valid m≤p: B=conv(h,w_neg,'full')[p].
- Third term h[p+m] valid m<n-p: F=corr(w_neg,h,'full')[n-1+p].
So G_neg[p] = prefix_w[p] - conv(h,w_neg,'full')[p] - corr(w_neg,h,'full')[n-1+p]. Good. For m=0 w_neg[0]=0, prefix[0]=0.
For positive, `G_pos[p] = reverse_prefix_w[p] - conv(h,w_pos,'full')[p] - corr(w_pos,h,'full')[n-1+p]`. Actually `pref_plus[p]=sum_{m=0}^{n-1-p} w_pos[m]` (suffix in w starting at 0), which can be `np.cumsum(w_pos[::-1])[::-1]`.
For negative, `pref_neg[p]=sum_{m=0}^p w_neg[m]` (prefix), `np.cumsum(w_neg)`.
Let's test n=2 negative w_neg=[0,w1]. prefix=[0, w1]. B=conv(h,w_neg)[:2]: conv = [0, h0*w1, h1*w1] first2 [0,h0w1]. F=corr(w_neg,h)[1:2]=[w1*h0 +0? output n-1=1: w0h0+w1h1? w0=0 =>h1w1; index2: w0h1=0]? Wait correlate(w_neg,h) w=[0,w1], h=[h0,h1] output [w1*h0,0*h0+w1*h1,0*h1] = [h0w1, h1w1,0]. tail [h1w1,0]. G_neg p0=0-0 -h1w1 = -h1w1. Direct negative m1 p0 derivative -h1*w1 correct. p1= w1 -h0w1 -0 = w1(1-h0) correct. Good.

Objective:
```
def smooth_obj(h,n,alpha):
  C=corr_full(h)
  m=C.max()
  # use m
  shifted=alpha*(C-m); mx=shifted.max(); lse=mx+log(sum(exp(shifted-mx)))
  return (m + lse/alpha)*dx
```

```
def project_sum(arr,target):
    x=np.clip(arr.astype(float,copy=True),0,1)
    cur=x.sum()
    if abs(cur-target)<1e-13: return x
    lo,hi = -x.min(), 1-x.max()
    # if target > cur, t>=0; if target <cur, t<=0
    def ... cannot closure; implement manually:
    if target>cur:
       lo=0.0; hi=1-x.max()
       # but if hi==0 and cur<target => infeasible (target>n) not
    else: lo=x.min(); hi=0.0
    # If hi-lo small? 80 iters
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(x+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target:
          if target>cur: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid? For target<cur, need decrease. Let's unify: We want s(mid) increasing in mid. If s(mid)<target, need increase mid: lo=mid regardless. If s(mid)>target, hi=mid. So simply:
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
But initial lo, hi must bracket. If target>cur: lo=0 s=cur<target, hi=max s at t=1-x.max? Could be <target? Since target<=n and x not all 1, hi gives some sum maybe target not reachable with uniform shift? But is uniform shift with clipping always enough to reach target? Suppose x=[0,0,1], target=1.5. hi=1-1=0? max at t=0? x sum=1, cannot reach 1.5 because only one 1 at bound and two zeros; uniform t=0 max (1,0,0) sum1. It can't reach 1.5; but actual projection to sum1.5 exists [0.5,0.5,1]. Uniform shift from x using `clip(x+u)` with same positive u: to increase zeros, need u=0.5 gives [0.5,0.5,1] sum2. But hi=1-x.max()=0 because max 1, so we didn't allow u=0.5? Because x.max=1, 1-x.max=0; but shifting entries below max by 0.5 still keeps max at 1 (<1) and new zeros to0.5, but the formula `hi=1-x.max` is not the right upper bound on uniform shift if some entries equal 1. If x has entries at 1, uniform positive shift would exceed 1 for them unless we also not shift them? def run_sym_slsq(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
  m=n//2
  z=project_sum(h[:m], m/2)
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sym_eq_fun,'jac':sym_eq_jac}]
  try:
    res=minimize(sym_obj,z,args=(alpha,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15})
  except: return None
  z=project_sum(res.x,m/2)
  full=make_sym_full(z,n)
  full=project_sum(full,n/2)
  return full, compute_c5(full,n)
```

def dct_init(n, coeff_list, rng?):
  m=n//2; j=np.arange(m); z=np.full(m,0.5)
  for k,a in coeff_list:
     z += a * np.cos(np.pi * k * (2*j+1)/(2*m))
  z=np.clip(z,0,1)
  full=np.empty(n); full[:m]=z; full[m:]=z[::-1]
  return project_sum(full,n/2)
```

def run_sym_slsq(h_full, n, alpha, maxiter):
    m=n//2
    z = (h_full[:m] + h_full[m:][::-1])*0.5 # but h_full maybe sym; if not, this averages halves reversed.
    z = project_sum(z, 0.5*m)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * m
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':sym_eq_fun,'jac':sym_eq_jac}]
    try:
      res = minimize(sym_obj, z, args=(alpha,), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15})
      if res.success or res.x is not None: z=res.x
    except Exception:
      pass
    z=project_sum(np.asarray(z,float),0.5*m)
    return make_sym_full(z,n), compute_c5(make...)
```

Let's implement:
```
def coord_setup(p,h,n,c):
    m_min=max(0,n-1-p); m_max=min(2*n-2, 2*n-2-p)
    ms=np.arange(m_min,m_max+1)
    jf=p+ms-(n-1); jb=p-ms+(n-1)
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if vf.any(): a[vf] += (1-h[jf[vf]])
    if vb.any(): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    diag_i=np.where(ms==n-1)[0]
    unaff=0.0
    if m_min>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[:m_min])))
    if m_max<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[m_max+1:])))
    return ms,a,diag_i,unaff
```

Let's draft final code mentally:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')) * (2.0/n))

def project_sum(x,target):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0.0,1.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-13:
        return x
    lo=-float(x.max())
    hi=1.0-float(x.min())
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       sv=np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if sv<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)

def make_sym(z,n=None):
    m=len(z)
    if n is None: n=2*m
    full=np.empty(n)
    full[:m]=z
    full[m:]=z[::-1]
    return full
```
For n even always. If m=0? no.

`symmetrize_full(h,n)`:
```
def symmetrize_full(h,n):
  m=n//2
  z=0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1])
  z=project_sum(z,0.5*m)
  return make_sym(z,n)
```
If n odd? Maybe:
```
if n%2:
  m=n//2
  z=0.5*(h[:m]+h[n-1:n-m-1:-1]) ... plus middle? 
```

```
def symmetrize_full(h,n):
  rev=h[::-1]
  g=0.5*(h+rev)
  g=project_sum(g,n/2.0)
  # then enforce mirror using half? If n even okay; if odd, maybe full mirror by half and middle
  if n%2==0:
    return make_sym(project_sum(g[:n//2],n/4.0),n)
  else:
    m=n//2; z=project_sum(g[:m],n/4.0-0.5*g[m])? 
```

`interpolate_h`:
```
def interpolate_h(src, n_src, n_tgt):
 if n_tgt==0: return ...
 xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_src,endpoint=False)
 xt=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_tgt,endpoint=False)
 return project_sum(np.interp(xt,xs,src),0.5*n_tgt)
```

Smooth:
```
def smooth_obj(x,alpha):
 n=len(x); C=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full'); m=C.max(); sh=alpha*(C-m); mx=sh.max(); return float((m+(mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha)*(2.0/n))
```
Need if all e underflow? mx zero because sh-max <=0, sum>=1. Good.

Gradient:
```
def grad_pos(w,h):
 n=len(h)
 return np.cumsum(w[::-1])[::-1] - np.convolve(h,w,'full')[:n] - np.correlate(w,h,'full')[n-1:]
def grad_neg(w,h):
 n=len(h)
 return np.cumsum(w) - np.convolve(h,w,'full')[:n] - np.correlate(w,h,'full')[n-1:]
def smooth_jac(x,alpha):
 n=len(x); C=...; m=C.max(); sh=alpha*(C-m); mx=sh.max(); e=np.exp(sh-mx); w=e/e.sum()
 wpos=w[n-1:].copy()
 wneg=np.zeros(n); wneg[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
 return (2.0/n/alpha)*(grad_pos(wpos,h)+grad_neg(wneg,h))
```
Need pass h to grad; `x` as h. Good.

```
def sym_eq_fun(x): return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))
def sym_eq_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x))
def sym_obj(x,alpha):
    h=make_sym(x,2*len(x)); return smooth_obj(h,alpha)
def sym_jac(x,alpha):
    h=make_sym(x,2*len(x)); g=smooth_jac(h,alpha); return g[:len(x)]+g[len(x):][::-1]
```

Full wrappers:
```
def full_eq_fun(x): return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))
def full_eq_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x))
def full_obj(x,alpha): return smooth_obj(x,alpha)
def full_jac(x,alpha): return smooth_jac(x,alpha)
```

`slsqp_sym(h_full,n,alpha,maxiter)`:
```
def slsqp_sym(h_full,n,alpha,maxiter):
  m=n//2
  z=project_sum(0.5*(h_full[:m]+h_full[m:][::-1]),0.5*m)
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sym_eq_fun,'jac':sym_eq_jac}]
  try:
    res=minimize(sym_obj,z,args=(alpha,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15})
    z=np.asarray(res.x,float)
  except Exception:
    res=None
  z=project_sum(z,0.5*m)
  full=project_sum(make_sym(z,n),0.5*n)
  return full, compute_c5(full,n)
```
If n odd, m floor; full length 2m not n, bad. Use only if even. In run, check.

CD:
```
def coord_setup(p,h,n,c): ...
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,diag_i,unaff): ...
def cd_run(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,show_random=False):
    h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); target=0.5*n
    seq=np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       order=rng.permutation(n) if show_random else seq
       improved=False
       block=0
       for p in order:
          h0=h[p]
          if h0<=1e-13 or h0>=1.0-1e-13: continue
          ms,a,diag_i,unaff=coord_setup(p,h,n,c)
          xn=solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,diag_i,unaff)
          if abs(xn-h0)>1e-13:
             delta=xn-h0; c[ms]+=delta*a
             if diag_i.size: c[ms[diag_i[0]]]-=delta*delta
             h[p]=xn; improved=True
             block+=1
             if block>=64:
                if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
                   h=project_sum(h,target); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
                block=0
       if not improved: break
       if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
          h=project_sum(h,target); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    h=project_sum(h,target)
    return h, compute_c5(h,n)
```
Need `diag_i.size` works if diag_i array. If `diag_i` empty? For n>=1, contains zero lag? Let's ensure for p arbitrary, ms includes n-1; yes. But safe.

`coord_setup` uses c for unaff. If c length 2n-1. For n=1? no.

`solve_coord`: copy previous but no project. We can simplify final x selection. Let's reproduce accurately.
```
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,diag_i,unaff):
    c_base=c[ms]
    cur_max=float(np.max(c))
    neg_mask=a<-1e-14; pos_mask=a>1e-14; zero_mask=np.abs(a)<=1e-14
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=cur_max
    has_diag=diag_i.size>0
    for _ in range(45):
       if Lhi-Llo<1e-12: break
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi); feasible=True
       if np.any(zero_mask) and np.any(c_base[zero_mask]>L+1e-12): feasible=False
       if feasible and np.any(neg_mask):
          xlo=max(0.0,float(np.max((L-c_base[neg_mask])/a[neg_mask])))
       else: xlo=0.0
       if feasible and np.any(pos_mask):
          xhi=min(1.0,float(np.min((L-c_base[pos_mask])/a[pos_mask])))
       else: xhi=1.0
       if xlo>xhi+1e-12: feasible=False
       if feasible and has_diag:
          di=diag_i[0]
          xd = c_base[di]
          ad = a[di]
          disc=ad*ad + 4.0*(xd-L)
          if disc>=0:
             sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(ad-sq)/2.0; r2=(ad+sq)/2.0
             # current h0
             h0=h[p]
             if xlo > h0 + r1 and xhi < h0 + r2:
                feasible=False
       if feasible: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
    if np.any(neg_mask): xlo=max(xlo,float(np.max((L-c_base[neg_mask])/a[neg_mask])))
    if np.any(pos_mask): xhi=min(xhi,float(np.min((L-c_base[pos_mask])/a[pos_mask])))
    xlo=max(0.0,xlo); xhi=min(1.0,xhi)
    if has_diag:
       di=diag_i[0]; xd=c_base[di]; ad=a[di]; h0=h[p]
       disc=ad*ad+4.0*(xd-L)
       if disc>=0:
          sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(ad-sq)/2.0; r2=(ad+sq)/2.0
          if xlo <= h0+r1:
             xhi=min(xhi,h0+r1)
          elif xhi >= h0+r2:
             xlo=max(xlo,h0+r2)
          else:
             d1=abs(xlo-(h0+r1)); d2=abs(xhi-(h0+r2))
             if d1<=d2: xhi=min(xhi,h0+r1)
             else: xlo=max(xlo,h0+r2)
    if xlo>xhi: return float(np.clip(0.5*(xlo+xhi),0,1))? Previous returned h0. Better return midpoint clipped? To stay feasible maybe choose closest? If infeasible due numerical, return h0.
    return float(np.clip(0.5*(xlo+xhi),0,1))
```

```
def slsqp_generic(x0, obj, jac, eqfun, eqjac, alpha, maxiter):
  nvar=len(x0); bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*nvar
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eqfun,'jac':eqjac}]
  try: res=minimize(obj,x0,args=(alpha,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15})
  except: return x0
  return np.asarray(res.x,float)
```

def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,diag_i,unaff):
    cb=c[ms]; lo=unaff; hi=float(c.max())
    nm=a<-1e-14; pm=a>1e-14; zm=nabs...; hd=diag_i.size
    for _ in range(45):
       L=(lo+hi)*0.5; ok=True
       if zm.any() and cb[zm].max()>L+1e-12: ok=False
       if ok:
          xl=0.0
          if nm.any(): xl=max(xl,float(((L-cb[nm])/a[nm]).max()))
          xh=1.0
          if pm.any(): xh=min(xh,float(((L-cb[pm])/a[pm]).min()))
          if xl>xh+1e-12: ok=False
          if ok and hd:
             ci=diag_i[0]; d=a[ci]*a[ci]+4*(cb[ci]-L)
             if d>=0:
                sq=np.sqrt(d); r1=(a[ci]-sq)*0.5; r2=(a[ci]+sq)*0.5
                if xl>h[p]+r1 and xh<h[p]+r2: ok=False
       if ok: hi=L
       else: lo=L
    L=hi; xl=0; xh=1; ... final ...
    return float(np.clip((xl+xh)*0.5,0,1)) if xl<=xh else h[p]

`coord_setup` compact:
```
def coord_setup(p,h,n,c):
    lo=max(0,n-1-p); hi=min(2*n-2,2*n-2-p); ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    jf=p+ms-(n-1); jb=p-ms+(n-1); vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms)); a[vf]+=1-h[jf[vf]]; a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    di=np.where(ms==n-1)[0]
    un=0.0
    if lo>0: un=float(c[:lo].max())
    if hi<2*n-2: un=max(un,float(c[hi+1:].max()))
    return ms,a,di,un
```

def dct_init(n, amps):
    m=n//2; j=np.arange(m); z=np.full(m,0.5)
    for k,amp in enumerate(amps,1):
       z += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*(2*j+1)/(2*m))
    z=np.clip(z,0,1); return project_sum(make_sym(z),n)

Let's draft code fully here to count approximate lines:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))

def project_sum(x,target):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    if abs(x.sum()-target)<1e-13: return x
    lo=-float(x.max()); hi=1.0-float(x.min())
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0).sum() < target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)

def make_sym(z,n=None):
    m=len(z)
    if n is None: n=2*m
    full=np.empty(n)
    full[:m]=z; full[m:]=z[::-1]
    return full
...
```
project_sum: For x all 0, target 175, lo=-0, hi=1; bisection. Good. For x all 1 target 175, lo=-1 hi=1; s(mid) monotone. Good.

`interpolate_h`: If n_tgt <=0? not.

`smooth_obj` and jac raw. Need `np.maximum`? Use m=C.max. If C contains nan? no.
```
def smooth_obj(h,alpha):
    C=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m=float(C.max())
    s=alpha*(C-m); mx=float(s.max())
    return float(m + (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx)))))/alpha)
```
If alpha<=0? We call positive.

`smooth_jac`:
```
def smooth_jac(h,alpha):
    C=...; m=C.max(); s=alpha*(C-m); mx=s.max(); e=np.exp(s-mx); w=e/e.sum()
    wpos=w[n-1:].copy(); wneg=np.zeros(n); wneg[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
    return (grad_pos(wpos,h)+grad_neg(wneg,h))/alpha
```
Potential overflow in `np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))`; s-mx<=0, finite. Good.

Grad functions use `np.cumsum(w[::-1])[::-1]`. For large n, creates arrays. Good.

`slsqp_generic`: If objective raises, return x0. Need check finite. If x0 maybe res.x not finite. Good.
```
def slsqp_generic(x0,obj,jac,eqfun,eqjac,alpha,maxiter):
    x0=np.asarray(x0,float); bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*len(x0)
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eqfun,'jac':eqjac}]
    try:
      res=minimize(obj,x0,args=(alpha,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15})
      x1=np.asarray(res.x,float)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(x1)): return x0
      return x1
    except Exception:
      return x0
```
No lambda.

Sym/Full wrappers:
```
def sym_obj(x,alpha): return smooth_obj(make_sym(x),alpha)
def sym_jac(x,alpha):
    h=make_sym(x); g=smooth_jac(h,alpha); m=len(x); return g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]
def sym_eq(x): return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))
def sym_eq_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x))
def full_obj(x,alpha): return smooth_obj(x,alpha)
def full_jac(x,alpha): return smooth_jac(x,alpha)
def full_eq(x): return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))
def full_eq_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x))
```
`make_sym(x)` returns len 2m; good.

`try_sym`:
```
def try_sym(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    m=n//2; z=project_sum(0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1]),0.5*m)
    z=slsqp_generic(z,sym_obj,sym_jac,sym_eq,sym_eq_jac,alpha,maxiter)
    z=project_sum(z,0.5*m)
    h=project_sum(make_sym(z,n),0.5*n)
    return h,compute_c5(h,n)
```
If n even; if m not? ok.

Pseudo-run code:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-5.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n=700; h=dct_init(n,0.25/np.arange(1,8))
    if n>1 and n%2:
       h=interpolate_h(h,n,n-1); n-=1
    if n<4:
       return np.full(n,0.5),0.5,n
    best_h=project_sum(h,0.5*n); best_n=n; best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    # sym
    hs=symmetrize_full(best_h,best_n); cs=...
    if cs<best_c: ...
    # Main SLSQP
    alphas=(50,500,5000,50000,200000)
    for a,mx in zip(alphas,(180,220,260,300,300)):
       if time.time()>deadline-300: break
       if best_n%2==0:
          h1,c1=try_sym(best_h,best_n,a,mx)
       else:
          h1,c1=try_full(best_h,best_n,a,mx)
       if c1<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    # CD cycles
    for _ in range(3):
       if time.time()>deadline-180: break
       h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,60,deadline-120,rng,False)
       if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
       if best_n%2==0 and time.time()<deadline-80:
          h1,c1=try_sym(best_h,best_n,50000,250)
          if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    # optional full slsqp
    if time.time()<deadline-250:
       h1,c1=try_full(best_h,best_n,5000,200)
       if c1<best_c: ...
       h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,40,deadline-120,rng,True)
       ...
    # exploration coarse
    if time.time()<deadline-350:
       for nc in (256,384):
          if nc>=best_n or time.time()>deadline-200: continue
          hc=interpolate_h(best_h,best_n,nc); hc=symmetrize_full(hc,nc)
          hc,cc=try_sym(hc,nc,5000,200) if nc%2==0 else try_full
          hc,cc=cd_run(hc,nc,40,deadline-120,rng,True)
          hup=interpolate_h(hc,nc,best_n); hup=symmetrize_full(hup,best_n)
          if best_n%2==0: hup,cup=try_sym(hup,best_n,50000,200)
          else: hup,cup=try_full
          hup,cup=cd_run(hup,best_n,50,deadline-80,rng,False)
          if cup<best_c: update
    # random dct starts
    if time.time()<deadline-300:
       for r in range(6):
          if time.time()>deadline-120: break
          K=min(6+2*r,best_n//2)
          amps=0.25*rng.normal(size=K)/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,K+1))
          h1=dct_init(best_n,amps)
          if best_n%2==0: h1,c1=try_sym(h1,best_n,2000,150)
          else: h1,c1=try_full
          h1,c1=cd_run(h1,best_n,30,deadline-60,rng,True)
          if c1<best_c: update
    # n variations
    cand=[]
    for nt in (best_n-100,best_n+50,800,600,900): add if valid.
    base=best_n
    for nt in [ ... ]:
       if time.time()>deadline-150: break
       nt=(nt//2)*2
       if nt<200 or nt>1000 or nt==base: continue
       h1=interpolate_h(best_h,best_n,nt); h1=symmetrize_full(h1,nt)
       if nt%2==0: h1,c1=try_sym(h1,nt,5000,200); h1,c1=cd_run(h1,nt,50,deadline-80,rng,True)
       if c1<best_c-1e-9: best_h,best_n,best_c=h1,nt,c1
    # final polish
    for _ in range(3):
       if time.time()>deadline-30: break
       if best_n%2==0:
          h1,c1=try_sym(best_h,best_n,200000,300)
          if c1<best_c: update
       h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,60,deadline-10,rng,False)
       if c1<best_c: update
       hs=symmetrize_full(best_h,best_n); c1=compute...
       if c1<best_c: update
       h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,30,deadline-5,rng,True)
       if c1<best_c: update
    best_h=project_sum(best_h,0.5*best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))

def project_sum(x,target):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    if abs(float(x.sum())-target)<1e-13:
        return x
    lo=-float(x.max()); hi=1.0-float(x.min())
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if float(np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0).sum())<target:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    return np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)

def make_sym(z,n=None):
    m=len(z)
    if n is None:
        n=2*m
    out=np.empty(n)
    out[:m]=z
    out[m:]=z[::-1]
    return out

def symmetrize_full(h,n):
    g=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    g=project_sum(g,0.5*n)
    if n%2==0:
        m=n//2
        z=project_sum(0.5*(g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]),0.5*m)
        return make_sym(z,n)
    return g

def interpolate_h(src,n_src,n_tgt):
    if n_tgt==n_src:
        return project_sum(src,0.5*n_src)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_src,endpoint=False)
    xt=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_tgt,endpoint=False)
    return project_sum(np.interp(xt,xs,src),0.5*n_tgt)

def smooth_obj(h,alpha):
    C=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m=float(C.max())
    s=alpha*(C-m)
    mx=float(s.max())
    return float(m+(mx+float(np.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))))))/alpha)

def grad_pos(w,h):
    return np.cumsum(w[::-1])[::-1]-np.convolve(h,w,'full')[:len(h)]-np.correlate(w,h,'full')[len(w)-1:]

def grad_neg(w,h):
    return np.cumsum(w)-np.convolve(h,w,'full')[:len(h)]-np.correlate(w,h,'full')[len(w)-1:]

def smooth_jac(h,alpha):
    n=len(h)
    C=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m=float(C.max())
    s=alpha*(C-m)
    mx=float(s.max())
    e=np.exp(s-mx)
    w=e/float(e.sum())
    wp=w[n-1:].copy()
    wn=np.zeros(n)
    wn[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
    return (grad_pos(wp,h)+grad_neg(wn,h))/alpha

def sym_obj(x,alpha):
    return smooth_obj(make_sym(x),alpha)

def sym_jac(x,alpha):
    h=make_sym(x)
    g=smooth_jac(h,alpha)
    m=len(x)
    return g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]

def sym_eq(x):
    return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))

def sym_eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones(len(x))

def full_obj(x,alpha):
    return smooth_obj(x,alpha)

def full_jac(x,alpha):
    return smooth_jac(x,alpha)

def full_eq(x):
    return float(x.sum()-0.5*len(x))

def full_eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones(len(x))

def slsqp_generic(x0,obj,jac,eqfun,eqjac,alpha,maxiter):
    x0=np.asarray(x0,dtype=float)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*len(x0)
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eqfun,'jac':eqjac}]
    try:
        res=minimize(obj,x0,args=(alpha,),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,
                     options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-14})
        x1=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x1)):
            return x0
        return x1
    except Exception:
        return x0

def try_sym(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    m=n//2
    z=project_sum(0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1]),0.5*m)
    z=slsqp_generic(z,sym_obj,sym_jac,sym_eq,sym_eq_jac,alpha,maxiter)
    z=project_sum(z,0.5*m)
    out=project_sum(make_sym(z,n),0.5*n)
    return out,compute_c5(out,n)

def try_full(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    x=project_sum(h,0.5*n)
    x=slsqp_generic(x,full_obj,full_jac,full_eq,full_eq_jac,alpha,maxiter)
    x=project_sum(x,0.5*n)
    return x,compute_c5(x,n)

def coord_setup(p,h,n,c):
    lo=max(0,n-1-p)
    hi=min(2*n-2,2*n-2-p)
    ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    jf=p+ms-(n-1)
    jb=p-ms+(n-1)
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf): a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    di=np.where(ms==n-1)[0]
    un=0.0
    if lo>0: un=float(c[:lo].max())
    if hi<2*n-2: un=max(un,float(c[hi+1:].max()))
    return ms,a,di,un

def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,di,un):
    cb=c[ms]
    Llo=un; Lhi=float(np.max(c))
    nm=a<-1e-14; pm=a>1e-14; zm=np.abs(a)<=1e-14
    hd=di.size>0
    for _ in range(40):
        L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
        ok=True
        if np.any(zm) and float(cb[zm].max())>L+1e-12:
            ok=False
        if ok:
            xl=0.0
            if np.any(nm): xl=max(xl,float(np.max((L-cb[nm])/a[nm])))
            xh=1.0
            if np.any(pm): xh=min(xh,float(np.min((L-cb[pm])/a[pm])))
            if xl>xh+1e-12: ok=False
            if ok and hd:
                ci=di[0]
                d=a[ci]*a[ci]+4.0*(cb[ci]-L)
                if d>=0.0:
                    qs=np.sqrt(d)
                    r1=0.5*(a[ci]-qs); r2=0.5*(a[ci]+qs)
                    if xl>h[p]+r1 and xh<h[p]+r2: ok=False
        if ok: Lhi=L
        else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    xl=0.0; xh=1.0
    if np.any(nm): xl=max(xl,float(np.max((L-cb[nm])/a[nm])))
    if np.any(pm): xh=min(xh,float(np.min((L-cb[pm])/a[pm])))
    xl=max(0.0,xl); xh=min(1.0,xh)
    if hd:
        ci=di[0]
        d=a[ci]*a[ci]+4.0*(cb[ci]-L)
        if d>=0.0:
            qs=np.sqrt(d)
            r1=0.5*(a[ci]-qs); r2=0.5*(a[ci]+qs)
            if xl<=h[p]+r1:
                xh=min(xh,h[p]+r1)
            elif xh>=h[p]+r2:
                xl=max(xl,h[p]+r2)
            else:
                if abs(xl-(h[p]+r1))<=abs(xh-(h[p]+r2)):
                    xh=min(xh,h[p]+r1)
                else:
                    xl=max(xl,h[p]+r2)
    if xl>xh:
        return float(h[p])
    return float(np.clip(0.5*(xl+xh),0.0,1.0))

def cd_run(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,random_order):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    target=0.5*n
    seq=np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(int(max_passes)):
        if time.time()>deadline:
            break
        order=rng.permutation(n) if random_order else seq
        improved=False
        for p in order:
            h0=float(h[p])
            if h0<=1e-13 or h0>=1.0-1e-13:
                continue
            ms,a,di,un=coord_setup(p,h,n,c)
            xn=solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a,di,un)
            if abs(xn-h0)>1e-13:
                delta=xn-h0
                c[ms]+=delta*a
                if di.size:
                    c[ms[di[0]]]-=delta*delta
                h[p]=xn
                improved=True
        if not improved:
            break
        if abs(float(h.sum())-target)>1e-9:
            h=project_sum(h,target)
        c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    h=project_sum(h,target)
    return h,compute_c5(h,n)

def dct_init(n,amps):
    m=n//2
    j=np.arange(m)
    z=np.full(m,0.5)
    K=min(len(amps),m)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
        z=float(0.0)??
```

```
def dct_init(n,amps):
    m=n//2
    if m==0: return np.array([0.5])
    j=np.arange(m)
    z=np.full(m,0.5)
    K=min(len(amps),m)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
        amp=float(amps[k-1])
        z=z+amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*(2*j+1)/(2.0*m))
    z=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    out=project_sum(make_sym(z),0.5*(2*m))
    if len(out)!=n and n%2: ...

Now, `run`: Need handle no parent and ensure even. If initial n odd, interpolate to n-1 (even). If n<4, set 600? For no parent even.
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-8.0
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try:
    h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=int(len(h))
  except Exception:
    n=700
    h=dct_init(n,0.25/np.arange(1,8))
  if n%2:
    n2=max(4,n-1); h=interpolate_h(h,n,n2); n=n2
  if n<=0:
     n=700; h=dct_init(n,0.25/np.arange(1,8))
  best_h=project_sum(h,0.5*n); best_n=n
  best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
  hs=symmetrize_full(best_h,best_n); cs=compute_c5(hs,best_n)
  if cs<best_c:
     best_h=hs; best_c=cs
  # main SLSQP
  main_alpha=(50.0,500.0,5000.0,50000.0)
  main_iter=(140,180,220,260)
  for idx in range(len(main_alpha)):
    if time.time()>deadline-350.0: break
    a=main_alpha[idx]
    if best_n%2==0:
       h1,c1=try_sym(best_h,best_n,a,main_iter[idx])
    else:
       h1,c1=try_full(best_h,best_n,a,main_iter[idx])
    if c1<best_c:
       best_h,best_c=h1,c1
  # cycles
  for _ in range(2):
    if time.time()>deadline-180.0: break
    h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,35,deadline-90.0,rng,False)
    if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    if best_n%2==0 and time.time()<deadline-50.0:
       h1,c1=try_sym(best_h,best_n,50000.0,220)
       if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
  # full SLSQP
  if time.time()<deadline-250.0:
    h1,c1=try_full(best_h,best_n,5000.0,180)
    if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
    h1,c1=cd_run(best_h,best_n,30,deadline-80.0,rng,True)
    if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
  # random starts
  if time.time()<deadline-300.0:
    for r in range(5):
       if time.time()>deadline-120.0: break
       K=min(6+2*r,best_n//2)
       amp=0.25*rng.normal(size=K)/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,K+1))
       h1=dct_init(best_n,amp)
       if best_n%2==0:
          h1,c1=try_sym(h1,best_n,2000.0,140)
       else:
          h1,c1=try_full(h1,best_n,2000.0,140)
       h1,c1=cd_run(h1,best_n,25,deadline-60.0,rng,True)
       if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
  # coarse
  if time.time()<deadline-350.0:
    for nc in (256,384):
       if nc>=best_n or time.time()>deadline-200.0: continue
       hc=interpolate_h(best_h,best_n,nc)
       hc=symmetrize_full(hc,nc)
       if nc%2==0:
          hc,cc=try_sym(hc,nc,5000.0,180)
       else:
          hc,cc=try_full(hc,nc,5000.0,180)
       hc,cc=cd_run(hc,nc,30,deadline-100.0,rng,True)
       hup=interpolate_h(hc,nc,best_n); hup=symmetrize_full(hup,best_n)
       if best_n%2==0:
          hup,cup=try_sym(hup,best_n,50000.0,180)
       else:

`project_sum`:
```
def project_sum(x,tg):
 x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0,1)
 if abs(x.sum()-tg)<1e-13: return x
 lo=-x.max(); hi=1-x.min()
 for _ in range(70):
  md=(lo+hi)*.5
  if np.clip(x+md,0,1).sum()<tg: lo=md
  else: hi=md
 return np.clip(x+(lo+hi)*.5,0,1)
```
Need `float` for max? NumPy scalar okay. In compare, sum returns np.float64. fine.

`make_sym`:
```
def make_sym(z,n=0):
 m=len(z); n=n or 2*m; o=np.empty(n); o[:m]=z; o[m:]=z[::-1]; return o
```
`sym_full`:
```
def sym_full(h,n):
 g=project_sum(.5*(h+h[::-1]),.5*n)
 if n%2==0:
  m=n//2; return make_sym(project_sum(.5*(g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]),.5*m),n)
 return g
```

`interp`:
```
def interp_h(a,ns,nt):
 if nt==ns: return project_sum(a,.5*ns)
 xs=np.linspace(0,2,ns,endpoint=False); xt=np.linspace(0,2,nt,endpoint=False)
 return project_sum(np.interp(xt,xs,a),.5*nt)
```

def smooth_obj(h,alpha):
 C=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); m=C.max(); s=alpha*(C-m); mx=s.max(); return float(m+(mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))))/alpha)

`smooth_jac`:
```
def smooth_jac(h,alpha):
 n=len(h); C=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); m=C.max(); s=alpha*(C-m); mx=s.max(); e=np.exp(s-mx); w=e/e.sum()
 wp=w[n-1:].copy(); wn=np.zeros(n); wn[1:]=w[:n-1][::-1]
 return (gp(wp,h)+gn(wn,h))/alpha
```

slsqp generic and sym wrappers compact:
```
def sym_obj(x,a): return smooth_obj(make_sym(x),a)
def sym_jac(x,a):
 h=make_sym(x); g=smooth_jac(h,a); m=len(x); return g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]
def sym_eq(x): return x.sum()-len(x)/2.0
def sym_eq_j(x): return np.ones(len(x))
def slsqp_sym(h,n,a,mx):
 m=n//2; z=project_sum(.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1]),.5*m)
 b=[(0,1)]*m
 cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sym_eq,'jac':sym_eq_j}]
 try:
  r=minimize(sym_obj,z,args=(a,),method='SLSQP',bounds=b,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':mx,'ftol':1e-14})
  z=np.asarray(r.x,float)
 except Exception: pass
 z=project_sum(z,.5*m); o=project_sum(make_sym(z,n),.5*n); return o,compute_c5(o,n)
```
This eliminates generic and try function. Good. No full.

`dct_init` compact:
```
def dct_init(n,amp):
 m=n//2; j=np.arange(m); z=np.full(m,.5)
 for k in range(1,min(len(amp),m)+1): z=z+amp[k-1]*np.cos(np.pi*k*(2*j+1)/(2*m))
 return project_sum(make_sym(np.clip(z,0,1)),.5*n)
```
Need if m=0? no.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_sum(x, tgt):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - tgt) < 1e-13:
        return x
    lo = -float(x.max())
    hi = 1.0 - float(x.min())
    for _ in range(72):
        md = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x + md, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) < tgt:
            lo = md
        else:
            hi = md
    return np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

def make_sym(z, n=0):
    m = len(z)
    n = int(n or 2 * m)
    o = np.empty(n)
    o[:m] = z
    o[m:] = z[::-1]
    return o

def sym_full(h, n):
    g = project_sum(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), 0.5 * n)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        m = n // 2
        z = project_sum(0.5 * (g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]), 0.5 * m)
        return make_sym(z, n)
    return g

def interp_h(src, ns, nt):
    if nt == ns:
        return project_sum(src, 0.5 * ns)
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ns, endpoint=False)
    xt = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nt, endpoint=False)
    return project_sum(np.interp(xt, xs, src), 0.5 * nt)

def smooth_obj(h, alpha):
    C = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = float(C.max())
    s = alpha * (C - m)
    mx = float(s.max())
    return float(m + (mx + float(np.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(s - mx)))))) / alpha)

def grad_pos(w, h):
    n = len(h)
    return np.cumsum(w[::-1])[::-1] - np.convolve(h, w, 'full')[:n] - np.correlate(w, h, 'full')[n - 1:]

def grad_neg(w, h):
    n = len(h)
    return np.cumsum(w) - np.convolve(h, w, 'full')[:n] - np.correlate(w, h, 'full')[n - 1:]

def smooth_jac(h, alpha):
    n = len(h)
    C = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = float(C.max())
    s = alpha * (C - m)
    mx = float(s.max())
    e = np.exp(s - mx)
    w = e / float(e.sum())
    wp = w[n - 1:].copy()
    wn = np.zeros(n)
    wn[1:] = w[:n - 1][::-1]
    return (grad_pos(wp, h) + grad_neg(wn, h)) / alpha

def sym_obj(x, alpha):
    return smooth_obj(make_sym(x), alpha)

def sym_jac(x, alpha):
    h = make_sym(x)
    g = smooth_jac(h, alpha)
    m = len(x)
    return g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]

def sym_eq(x):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * len(x))

def sym_eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones(len(x))

def slsqp_sym(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    m = n // 2
    z = project_sum(0.5 * (h[:m] + h[m:][::-1]), 0.5 * m)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sym_eq, 'jac': sym_eq_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(sym_obj, z, args=(alpha,), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                       constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-14})
        z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z = h[:m]
    except Exception:
        pass
    z = project_sum(z, 0.5 * m)
    h = project_sum(make_sym(z, n), 0.5 * n)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n)

def coord_setup(p, h, n, c):
    lo = max(0, n - 1 - p)
    hi = min(2 * n - 2, 2 * n - 2 - p)
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf):
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb):
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    di = np.where(ms == n - 1)[0]
    un = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        un = float(c[:lo].max())
    if hi < 2 * n - 2:
        un = max(un, float(c[hi + 1:].max()))
    return ms, a, di, un

def solve_coord(c, h, p, n, ms, a, di, un):
    cb = c[ms]
    Llo = un
    Lhi = float(np.max(c))
    lin = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
    if di.size:
        lin[di] = False
    nm = lin & (a < -1e-14)
    pm = lin & (a > 1e-14)
    lm = lin & (np.abs(a) <= 1e-14)
    hd = di.size > 0
    for _ in range(42):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        ok = True
        if np.any(lm) and float(cb[lm].max()) > L + 1e-12:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            xl = 0.0
            if np.any(nm):
                xl = max(xl, float(np.max((L - cb[nm]) / a[nm])))
            xh = 1.0
            if np.any(pm):
                xh = min(xh, float(np.min((L - cb[pm]) / a[pm])))
            if xl > xh + 1e-12:
                ok = False
            if ok and hd:
                ci = di[0]
                d = a[ci] * a[ci] + 4.0 * (cb[ci] - L)
                if d >= 0.0:
                    q = np.sqrt(d)
                    r1 = 0.5 * (a[ci] - q)
                    r2 = 0.5 * (a[ci] + q)
                    if xl > h[p] + r1 and xh < h[p] + r2:
                        ok = False
        if ok:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L
    L = Lhi
    xl = 0.0
    xh = 1.0
    if np.any(nm):
        xl = max(xl, float(np.max((L - cb[nm]) / a[nm])))
    if np.any(pm):
        xh = min(xh, float(np.min((L - cb[pm]) / a[pm])))
    xl = max(0.0, xl)
    xh = min(1.0, xh)
    if hd:
        ci = di[0]
        d = a[ci] * a[ci] + 4.0 * (cb[ci] - L)
        if d >= 0.0:
            q = np.sqrt(d)
            r1 = 0.5 * (a[ci] - q)
            r2 = 0.5 * (a[ci] + q)
            if xl <= h[p] + r1:
                xh = min(xh, h[p] + r1)
            elif xh >= h[p] + r2:
                xl = max(xl, h[p] + r2)
            else:
                if abs(xl - (h[p] + r1)) <= abs(xh - (h[p] + r2)):
                    xh = min(xh, h[p] + r1)
                else:
                    xl = max(xl, h[p] + r2)
    if xl > xh:
        return float(h[p])
    return float(np.clip(0.5 * (xl + xh), 0.0, 1.0))

def cd_run(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, random_order):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    tgt = 0.5 * n
    seq = np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(int(max_passes)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else seq
        improved = False
        for p in order:
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
                continue
            ms, a, di, un = coord_setup(p, h, n, c)
            xn = solve_coord(c, h, p, n, ms, a, di, un)
            if abs(xn - h0) > 1e-13:
                d = xn - h0
                c[ms] += d * a
                if di.size:
                    c[ms[di[0]]] -= d * d
                h[p] = xn
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        if abs(float(h.sum()) - tgt) > 1e-9:
            h = project_sum(h, tgt)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    h = project_sum(h, tgt)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n)

def dct_init(n, amp):
    m = n // 2
    j = np.arange(m)
    z = np.full(m, 0.5)
    K = min(len(amp), m)
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        z = z + float(amp[k - 1]) * np.cos(np.pi * k * (2 * j + 1) / (2.0 * m))
    return project_sum(make_sym(np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0), n), 0.5 * n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        h = dct_init(n, 0.25 / np.arange(1, 8))
    on = n
    if n % 2:
        n = max(6, n - 1)
        h = interp_h(h, on, n)
    if n < 6:
        n = 700
        h = dct_init(n, 0.25 / np.arange(1, 8))

    best_n = n
    best_h = project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    hs = sym_full(best_h, best_n)
    cs = compute_c5(hs, best_n)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    h1, c1 = cd_run(best_h, best_n, 22, dl - 600.0, rng, True)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    for a, mi in ((50.0, 140), (500.0, 180), (5000.0, 220), (50000.0, 260)):
        if time.time() > dl - 360.0:
            break
        h1, c1 = slsqp_sym(best_h, best_n, a, mi)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    for _ in range(2):
        if time.time() > dl - 180.0:
            break
        h1, c1 = cd_run(best_h, best_n, 38, dl - 80.0, rng, False)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        if time.time() < dl - 50.0:
            h1, c1 = slsqp_sym(best_h, best_n, 50000.0, 230)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < dl - 250.0:
        h1, c1 = cd_run(best_h, best_n, 32, dl - 80.0, rng, True)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < dl - 320.0:
        for r in range(5):
            if time.time() > dl - 130.0:
                break
            K = min(8 + 2 * r, best_n // 2)
            amp = 0.25 * rng.normal(size=K) / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, K + 1))
            h1 = dct_init(best_n, amp)
            h1, c1 = slsqp_sym(h1, best_n, 2000.0, 150)
            h1, c1 = cd_run(h1, best_n, 28, dl - 60.0, rng, True)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < dl - 320.0:
        for nt in (best_n - 100, best_n + 50, 800, 600, 900):
            if time.time() > dl - 150.0:
                break
            nt = int(nt)
            if nt % 2:
                nt -= 1
            if nt < 200 or nt > 1000 or nt == best_n:
                continue
            h1 = interp_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
            h1 = sym_full(h1, nt)
            h1, c1 = slsqp_sym(h1, nt, 5000.0, 190)
            h1, c1 = cd_run(h1, nt, 38, dl - 70.0, rng, True)
            if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h1, nt, c1

    for _ in range(2):
        if time.time() > dl - 25.0:
            break
        h1, c1 = slsqp_sym(best_h, best_n, 200000.0, 240)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        h1, c1 = cd_run(best_h, best_n, 44, dl - 5.0, rng, False)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1
        hs = sym_full(best_h, best_n)
        c1 = compute_c5(hs, best_n)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, c1
        h1, c1 = cd_run(best_h, best_n, 22, dl - 1.0, rng, True)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    best_h = project_sum(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
